1,465 entries, acl-dryrun to add-endpoint. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **acl-dryrun**: Simulates the execution of a command by a user, without executing the command. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-dryrun.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-genpass**: Generates a pseudorandom, secure password that can be used to identify ACL users. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-genpass.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-getuser**: Lists the ACL rules of a user. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-getuser.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-help**: Returns helpful text about the different subcommands. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-help.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-list**: Dumps the effective rules in ACL file format. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-list.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-load**: Reloads the rules from the configured ACL file. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-load.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-log**: Lists recent security events generated due to ACL rules. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-log.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-save**: Saves the effective ACL rules in the configured ACL file. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-save.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-setuser**: Creates and modifies an ACL user and its rules. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-setuser.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-users**: Lists all ACL users. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-users.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **acl-whoami**: Returns the authenticated username of the current connection. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/acl-whoami.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)

- **add**: A session task with no check needs --why saying why no check decides it (except --inspect). Add a task to the session run; its start snapshot is taken now.
